# SMH Forward Proof-Schedule Monitor v0 (smh_gates.py)

Authored 14 Nov 2026 (session 68, PROGRESS block 76). Forward-track
tooling only: NO new hypothesis, NO new evaluation, NO new statistic. The
tool turns the proof schedule that was pre-declared BEFORE the first
forward row (docs/smh_proof_criteria_v0.md, sessions 44/46; constants
frozen in smh_theorem.py's PROOF/READOUT blocks) into a report-only
instrument that applies it mechanically to the forward log
(data/smh_forward_log.csv). Every verdict it prints is a mechanical
restatement of a frozen rule. A verdict is bookkeeping, never an
inferential claim.

## 1. What it does

- `status` - the whole schedule against the real log. Absent/empty log =
  the honest EMPTY state: all gates NOT-ARMED, the schedule printed in
  full (this is the current live state; the log is empty by design and
  the first row waits for the market's next compression/stress cross).
- `selftest` - in-memory boundary fixtures (stdlib only; no network, no
  bundle access). EXECUTED 14 Nov 2026: PASS, before and after a one
  dead-branch cleanup in readout_check (the redundant
  `br_upper = upper if ... else upper` collapsed to the honest comment
  that the Brier threshold shares the logloss win side per claim).
- `check` - deferred constants parity: asserts the embedded constants
  equal smh_theorem's frozen ones (23 constant groups; needs the
  scientific stack). MUST pass on the next Python-enabled reset BEFORE
  the first live Sunday use of the status verdicts.

## 2. The schedule implemented (semantics frozen here)

- OCCURRENCE GATES: counted on RESOLVED 12-week rows in log order, per
  claim kind (compression_first_event, stress_de_stress), evaluated at
  the EXACT milestones only (n = 10/20/40/60): KILLED if events K <=
  kill line (3/8/19/31, both kinds), ALIVE otherwise; the claim status is
  STICKY (once KILLED at any gate it stays KILLED; no interpolation
  between milestones). Rows of the other kind never leak in; unresolved
  rows never count.
- DIRECTION PEEKS: counted on resolved forward first events (event_12w
  == 1 with dir_12w filled), examined at exactly n = 40 and n = 60:
  promoted at up >= 27/40 then 39/60; down-bias annotation at up <= 13/40
  or 21/60; otherwise NO-ACTION and direction stays refused.
- READOUTS: at exactly n = 26/52/104 resolved windows per claim, the
  fixed-forecast thresholds on the event count (sessions 44/46
  arithmetic): comp_12w logloss K >= 15/29/57, Brier K >= 14/28/56;
  comp_4w both 6/12/23; stress_12w both 15/30/60; stress_4w FLIPPED side
  logloss K <= 9/19/39, Brier K <= 10/20/40. THE POSITIVE PROOF =
  comp_12w at 104 windows with BOTH rules met (K >= 57).
- CALIBRATION CONTEXT (descriptive, never a test): realized mean
  brier_12w/logloss_12w against the frozen CALIB/SCALIB expectations.
- SEDUCTIVE-CELL REGISTER: printed as the closed three-cell register
  with its frozen definitions; cells are examined only at >= 10 forward
  rows in the cell (counts reported by status; promotion is a doc action
  under the pre-declared CP95 rule, never automated here).

## 3. Validation (two independent implementations, lesson 14)

- The real Python selftest: PASS (executed 14 Nov 2026, stdlib Python
  3.12; the tool needs no pandas/numpy/scipy for status/selftest by
  design, so its first run did not wait for a stack-enabled reset).
- The independent TS mirror smh_gates_ts_mirror.ts: 30/30 fixtures PASS
  (smh_gates_report.json), re-implementing the same boundary arithmetic
  from the same frozen constants: every gate boundary pair (K = kill
  KILLED / K = kill+1 ALIVE at n=10/20/40/60, both kinds), sticky kill,
  the readout boundaries including the positive-proof count (comp 104
  K=57 BOTH, K=56 brier-only) and the stress_4w flipped side (K=39 both,
  K=40 brier-only, K=41 both lose), the direction peek boundaries
  (27/26/13 of 40; 39 of 60), kind isolation, unresolved exclusion and
  threshold monotonicity.

## 4. Wiring

- verify_all.sh: wired as its 25th check (the selftest invocation;
  stdlib-only, so the check runs wherever the chain runs). The chain was
  at 24 checks ALL PASS as last verified; the first full 25-check chain
  run happens on the next Python-enabled reset (with the stack) together
  with the deferred `smh_gates.py check` parity pass and the pending
  climato/entrydep pandas first runs inside smh_robust.
- Sunday cadence: NOT accelerated or pre-empted. When a Sunday session
  exists, the monitor's status is a report-only readout AFTER smh_forecast
  /smh_score; it never books, never drafts, never writes.

## 5. Honest computation log

- The selftest's first execution PASSED first pass; one dead branch
  (`br_upper = upper if claim in ("stress_4w",) else upper`, a tautology)
  was found by inspection of the readout_check code and collapsed to an
  honest comment BEFORE the TS mirror was written; selftest re-run PASS.
  No fixture, constant or verdict changed.
- The TS mirror's first run: 30/30 PASS first pass; no corrections.
- Nothing outside the schedule was computed; the real log was never
  touched (it does not exist yet); no forward statistics exist; the
  holdout is spent and was not read.

## 6. Discipline

Kill lines count RESOLVED ROWS, never calendar years. The log is
append-only. The tool writes nothing. Verdicts are bookkeeping. Paper
trading only. The Amendment 9/10 decision requests and all standing
Andrea items are unchanged by this tooling.

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/docs/smh_hazard_v0.md (11846 bytes)
# SMH Hazard Layer v0 (The Salient Market Hypothesis, layer 3)

Status: DESIGN BOOKKEEPING on the UNSEALED pre-2021 sample only (rule-set-V class). Date filter date < 2021-01-01 throughout; n = 794 decision weeks; holdout 2021+ never touched. Nothing here is an inferential claim, a tested model, or a trade. The numbers below are the framework's first measured hazard table, frozen as designed; the forward protocol (section 5) is the only path by which any of them earns trust.

Named 12 Nov 2026 by Andrea: the framework formerly logged as "the State-Clock Framework" is now the Salient Market Hypothesis (SMH). Same object, same falsification rules, new name. The canvas folder name is unchanged (a platform convention); the title and all future references say SMH.

## 1. What this layer adds

The framework canvas defines the clock layer as tercile tables (the 4-week event |r4| >= 2.5 sigma_w, hazard 0.468 low tercile vs 0.143 high). This document adds the TIME dimension at the entry level: conditioning on a compression ENTRY (the cross into the low tercile), what is the hazard of the first significant 1-week event as a function of weeks since entry, and which way does it resolve. This is discrete survival analysis: Kaplan-Meier hazards of a first event, direction split of first events by entry conditions. The mathematics is the same spine as clinical time-to-event methodology (Kaplan-Meier, and later Cox), which is the medicine bridge.

Definitions, frozen:

- Decision week t: a weekly close with sig_w = 8-week trailing weekly log-return std (ddof=1).
- Compression entry at t: sig_w(t) <= LOW (LOW = 0.01739, the frozen pre-2021 tercile cutpoint) AND sig_w(t-1) > LOW. A cross, not a dwell. True crosses pre-2021: n = 41.
- Significant 1-week event at t (event in week t+1): |r1(t+1)| >= 2.5 x sig_w(t). Threshold uses the decision week's vol (updated weekly, not frozen at entry; see caveats).
- First event: the earliest h in 1..12 such that the decision week t0+h-1 flags an event, with no earlier flagged decision week since entry. Episodes with any NaN or sample-end within the window are censored (excluded at that h, KM convention).

## 2. The frozen tables (pre-2021, unsealed)

Unconditional 1-week significant-week rate: 0.0448 (all decision weeks).
While in compression (all 289 low-tercile decision weeks, not just entries): 0.0969 (28/289), 2.2x unconditional. Naive binomial z ~ 4.3 vs the unconditional rate; NOT corrected for overlap/autocorrelation; design bookkeeping, not inference. This is the strongest simple statement in the layer: the compression state roughly doubles the weekly event hazard.

Kaplan-Meier discrete hazard of first 1-week significant event after a compression entry (n = 41 entries; table CORRECTED 12 Nov 2026, session 32, after an off-by-one in the risk-set logic was caught by the person-week reconstruction; see the computation log):

| h (weeks since entry) | at risk | events | hazard | cum event prob |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 41 | 2 | 0.049 | 0.049 |
| 2 | 39 | 3 | 0.077 | 0.122 |
| 3 | 36 | 3 | 0.083 | 0.195 |
| 4 | 33 | 3 | 0.091 | 0.268 |
| 5 | 30 | 3 | 0.100 | 0.341 |
| 6 | 27 | 4 | 0.148 | 0.439 |
| 7 | 23 | 2 | 0.087 | 0.488 |
| 8 | 21 | 0 | 0.000 | 0.488 |
| 9 | 21 | 1 | 0.048 | 0.512 |
| 10 | 20 | 1 | 0.050 | 0.537 |
| 11 | 19 | 2 | 0.105 | 0.585 |
| 12 | 17 | 2 | 0.118 | 0.634 |

Headline readings: 26 of 41 entries (0.634) produce a first significant 1-week event within 12 weeks; median lag to first event 5 weeks; the hazard RISES with time since entry through h ~ 6 (0.148, 4/27) before the small-n tail wobbles. A rising hazard with duration-in-state is the pattern a hazard clock predicts (time since compression entry carries information); with n = 41 it is SUGGESTIVE, not established. The h = 6 peak is 5 events; single-cell noise can do this.

Context cross-check (the clock study's 4-week event, measured from the entry week): 0.366 (15/41) of entries see a 4-week significant move in their first 4 weeks, consistent in magnitude with the frozen low-tercile table (0.468 measured over all low-tercile decision weeks) once the stricter entry-cross conditioning and the frozen-at-entry threshold are accounted for.

Direction of the FIRST significant week, by entry conditions:

| Entry condition | up | down | up rate | flag |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| all entries | 17 | 9 | 0.654 | small n |
| mom12 > 0 at entry | 11 | 8 | 0.579 | n = 19 |
| mom12 <= 0 at entry | 6 | 1 | 0.857 | n = 7, SEDUCTIVE CELL, flagged |
| state = reversal_down at entry | 9 | 3 | 0.750 | n = 12, suggestive |
| state = momentum_up at entry | 3 | 3 | 0.500 | n = 6, no signal |

(The two remaining entry states have 2 events each: reversal_up 2 up/0 down, momentum_down 1 up/1 down; too small to carry any content, recorded for completeness.)

The overall up-lean (17/9) is itself partly mechanical for a long-drift equity index and must not be read as SMH content. The interesting cell is the one the framework's own logic predicts: compression entered after a DOWN 12-week move resolves its first event UP (6 of 7 pre-2021). This is the same seductive shape as the clock study's reversal_up cell (0.385, n = 26, flagged there) and the same discipline applies: n = 7, selected after seeing the table's family, absolutely not trustworthy until it survives forward scoring. It is recorded because it is the concrete shape of "the trade no one sees" the hypothesis is hunting: a compressed market after decline, where the crowd anchors on the low vol and the down move, and the first significant week resolves upward. The forward protocol is the only honest test of whether that shape repeats.

## 3. Caveats, frozen with the tables

1. n = 41 entries in ~20 years. Every cell above is small-n; the direction splits doubly so.
2. Episode dependence: entries are crosses (no two entries inside one low-vol spell), but adjacent spells in the same regime are not independent. The naive z and all cell rates inherit this.
3. Mechanical threshold effect: sig_w is small at entry and the event threshold uses the UPDATED sig_w each week, so vol expansion raises the bar as it raises the moves; the rising hazard through h ~ 6 is partly this mechanical chase and partly genuine duration conditioning, and pre-2021 data cannot fully separate them. The forward protocol, scored against these frozen rows, can.
4. Selection: the direction split conditions (mom12 sign, AVFI state at entry) were chosen from the framework's structure and the clock study's family, but the specific cells were inspected before being recorded. Treat all cells as exploratory.
5. All numbers are unsealed pre-2021 design bookkeeping. No claim, no test, no trade.
6. Standing consistency check (adopted 12 Nov 2026 after the off-by-one): every KM-style table must be reconstructed from an independent person-week dataset, and the sum of at-risk counts must equal the person-week count, before the table is frozen or cited.

## 4. Computation log (honest bugs, kept)

Two bugs were found and fixed in the hazard computation this session, both recorded per the programme's honest-logging culture:

1. Prior-window censoring bug: the first implementation's prior-window check (t0+1 .. t0+h-1) included the horizon's own event week, so every episode with an event at h >= 2 was removed from the risk set by its own event before being counted. Symptom: hazard 0.049 at h = 1 then all zeros, with a draining at-risk pool. Fix: prior window is t0+1 .. t0+h-2 (empty at h <= 2).
2. Entry-detection dtype bug: `~` applied to a shifted boolean (object dtype) series produced -1/-2 integers, both truthy, so ALL 289 compression weeks were treated as entries instead of the 41 true crosses. Symptom: entry count 289 equal to the total compression-week count, and a suspiciously smooth cumulative curve. Fix: `.astype(bool)` before negation. Lesson, recorded for the lessons file: plausible-looking output is not correct output; the entry-count cross-check against the known 41 caught it.
3. Off-by-one risk-set bug in the FROZEN TABLE ITSELF (found 12 Nov 2026, session 32, while building the person-week dataset for the Cox layer; corrected above): the KM risk-set check used prior window t0+1 .. t0+h-2, but the h = 1 decision week is the entry week t0 itself, so episodes with an h = 1 event were never removed from later risk sets. Consequences: at-risk counts inflated after h = 1 (the original table summed 343 across horizons vs the true 327), one duplicate first event (27 vs 26), hazards slightly wrong (h = 6: 0.172/5 events vs corrected 0.148/4), cum 0.629 vs 0.634. Detection method, now a STANDING POLICY for every KM-style table: the sum of at-risk counts across horizons must equal the independently constructed person-week count; 343 vs 327 failed the check. What survived the correction unchanged: the h = 1 row, the rising-hazard shape, the median lag of 5 weeks, the compression-state rate doubling (not KM-based), the mom12- 6/7 seductive cell, and the reversal_down direction shape. The buggy table is preserved in the session 31 record; this doc carries only the corrected numbers plus this log entry.

## 5. Forward evaluation protocol (design; activation requires Andrea's signed, dated amendment only if inferential claims are wanted; descriptive rows cost nothing)

This subsumes the shelfed Amendment 8 clock-study shape without re-activating it; it is the SMH-native version:

1. Every Sunday session, when a compression entry has occurred (or a week is in compression), log a forecast row in advance: hazard of a significant week within 4 and within 12 weeks (from the frozen KM table), and the direction split conditional on entry state. Append-only, no backfilled rows, no table revision from post-2020 data ever.
2. Score with Brier and log scoring as the event windows complete; reliability readouts at 52 and 104 scored windows; no inferential claim before 104 windows.
3. Climatology rows (unconditional 0.0448, compression 0.0969) are scored alongside; the SMH layer earns its keep only by beating climatology on reliability.
4. No trades from this layer; no family alpha consumed; the layer is descriptive until a signed amendment says otherwise. The judgment log (Amendment 7, shelfed unsigned) remains the separate instrument for discretionary calls.

Instrument (shipped 12 Nov 2026, session 33, tooling only): research/smh_forecast.py drafts and guards these rows. Subcommands: status (latest week's clock state vs the frozen cut, descriptive only), draft (emits the forecast row if the latest decision week is a compression entry; refuses with a reason otherwise; the protocol logs entries, not noise), draft --append (append-only write to data/smh_forward_log.csv, guarded: Sunday decision cadence only, fresh entry weeks only, no duplicate entry weeks ever), and selftest, which recomputes the entire frozen KM and direction table set from the canonical pre-2021 data and asserts equality with the embedded constants, including the standing person-week consistency check. The selftest is wired into verify_all.sh as its 17th check. All live-week reading is limited to the descriptive entry question; no evaluation, no forward statistics, no trades. The scorer that fills the resolution columns (Brier/log per completed window) is the next build, written only after the first forward rows exist.

## 6. Standing data need

True "unseen" status of any signature is verifiable only against priced expectations. The layer's compression signature is partly priced (low implied vol costs little to observe). Registering the standing data need: an implied-vol series (VIX or VXN as a priced-expectations benchmark) to test whether SMH's compression hazard adds anything beyond what options markets already charge for. Documented, not actioned; no new data pulled this session.

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/docs/smh_hazard_v0.md

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/docs/smh_hazard_v01_cox.md (8627 bytes)
# SMH Hazard Layer v0.1: the Cox covariate build, and a correction to the v0 KM table

Status: DESIGN BOOKKEEPING on the UNSEALED pre-2021 sample only (rule-set-V
class), 12 Nov 2026. RECORDED AS SESSION 34 (renumbered from the build-time
self-label "session 32" after concurrent-session reconciliation: a
concurrent Python session recorded the same KM correction as session 32
(docs/smh_cox_v01.md) and the forward-protocol instrument as session 33;
this document is the INDEPENDENT TypeScript replication of the correction
and the naive-SE Cox fits, whose verbatim artifact run reproduced every
expected value). Date filter date < 2021-01-01 throughout;
41 compression entries; holdout 2021+ never touched. No inferential claim,
no test, no trade, no rule/default/hypothesis change. The v0 forward protocol
(section 5 of smh_hazard_v0.md) is UNCHANGED and remains the only path by
which any number here earns trust.

## 1. What this layer adds

The v0 layer (smh_hazard_v0.md) froze the Kaplan-Meier table of the first
significant 1-week event after compression entries and the direction splits.
This v0.1 layer is the pre-named next build: discrete-time Cox regression
(cloglog link, the Prentice discrete-time proportional-hazards form) on the
person-week expansion of the same 41 episodes, with entry-condition
covariates:

- duration h (weeks since entry, 1..12), the hazard clock's own variable;
- mom12neg = 1 if the 12-week log return at entry is <= 0 (the v0
  "seductive cell" 6/7);
- rev_down = 1 if the AVFI state at entry is reversal_down.

Models (all cloglog: log(-log(1 - lambda_h)) = X beta):
M00 intercept only; M0 = M00 + h; M1 = M0 + mom12neg; M2 = M0 + rev_down;
M3 = M0 + mom12neg + rev_down.

## 2. CORRECTION TO THE FROZEN v0 KM TABLE (bug #3, found this session)

Replicating the v0 pipeline from the stated frozen definitions and the
canonical weekly closes reproduces the 41 entries exactly but NOT the v0
at-risk column: the frozen table keeps BOTH h=1-event episodes in every
later risk set (at-risk = textbook + 2 uniformly from h=2), and one of them
(ENTRY 2013-03-01, state reversal_down) has a SECOND flagged event at h=6
that the frozen table counted as an additional first event. Diagnosis: the
v0 computation log's "fix" for bug #1 set the prior-event window to
decision weeks t0+1..t0+h-2, which EXCLUDES horizon 1's decision week
t0 — so h=1 events were never treated as prior events. The two h=1-event
episodes are 2013-03-01 and 2014-10-17. Everything else in the v0 pipeline
(entry detection, event flag, censoring) replicates exactly.

Textbook-corrected KM table (pre-2021, unsealed; replaces the v0 table for
all future reference; the v0 doc's table stays in place as the frozen
original with this correction noted):

| h | at risk | events | hazard | cum event prob |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 41 | 2 | 0.049 | 0.049 |
| 2 | 39 | 3 | 0.077 | 0.122 |
| 3 | 36 | 3 | 0.083 | 0.195 |
| 4 | 33 | 3 | 0.091 | 0.268 |
| 5 | 30 | 3 | 0.100 | 0.341 |
| 6 | 27 | 4 | 0.148 | 0.439 |
| 7 | 23 | 2 | 0.087 | 0.488 |
| 8 | 21 | 0 | 0.000 | 0.488 |
| 9 | 21 | 1 | 0.048 | 0.512 |
| 10 | 20 | 1 | 0.050 | 0.537 |
| 11 | 19 | 2 | 0.105 | 0.585 |
| 12 | 17 | 2 | 0.118 | 0.634 |

Corrected headline readings: 26 of 41 entries (0.634) see a first
significant 1-week event within 12 weeks (v0 said 27/41 = 0.659); median
lag 5 weeks (unchanged); the hazard still RISES through h ~ 6 but the peak
is 4/27 = 0.148 (v0 said 5/29 = 0.172); corrected direction splits: all
entries 17 up / 9 down (0.654, v0 said 18/9); reversal_down at entry
9 up / 3 down (v0 said 10/3); mom12 <= 0 at entry 6 up / 1 down
(UNCHANGED — the seductive cell survives correction and is then tested in
section 4); momentum_up 3/3 (unchanged). The v0 caveats all carry over
unchanged and with more force.

## 3. Person-week structure

41 episodes -> 327 person-weeks at risk, 26 event rows (the double-counted
2013-03-01 second event is NOT an event row here; KM-consistent). Episodes
are censored (excluded from that and later horizons) when the window exits
the pre-2021 sample; no episode was mid-window censored except by events.

## 4. The Cox fits (naive SEs; exploratory, design bookkeeping)

| model | terms | beta (naive SE) | naive z | loglik | LR vs base |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| M00 | intercept | -2.4907 (0.1962) | -12.70 | -90.7663 | — |
| M0 | + h | h: 0.0129 (0.0574) | 0.22 | -90.7413 | 0.05 (vs M00) |
| M1 | + mom12neg | 0.2806 (0.4424) | 0.63 | -90.549 | 0.38 (vs M0) |
| M2 | + rev_down | 0.5955 (0.3937) | 1.51 | -89.6304 | 2.22 (vs M0) |
| M3 | + both | mom12neg 0.1650 (0.4502); rev_down 0.5694 (0.4005) | 0.37 / 1.42 | -89.5648 | 2.35 (2 df, vs M0) |

Readings, stated honestly:

1. DURATION: the cloglog duration slope is 0.0129/week with naive z 0.22
   and LR 0.05 vs a constant hazard. Once modeled smoothly, the v0 table's
   "hazard rises through h ~ 6" is indistinguishable from a CONSTANT
   weekly hazard at this n. The rising profile was a table shape, not a
   measurable duration trend. The hazard-clock hypothesis is correspondingly
   weaker than the v0 doc's "suggestive" framing.
2. mom12neg: beta 0.28, z 0.63, LR 0.38. The 6/7 "trade no one sees" cell
   adds NOTHING once the episode structure is respected: the model can
   reproduce the cell from base rates plus noise. Its status drops from
   "seductive but flagged" to "consistent with no effect at this n". It is
   NOT closed (nothing is closed by a null LR at n=41 with naive SEs), but
   it earns no confidence either; only forward scoring can move it.
3. rev_down: beta 0.60, z 1.51, LR 2.22 — the largest of the three, still
   nowhere near any evidential bar even BEFORE the episode-dependence and
   selection caveats (which push all of these further from significance).
4. All naive SEs UNDERSTATE uncertainty (episode dependence, small n,
   covariate selection after inspecting the v0 cells). No p-value is claimed
   anywhere in this document; the LR columns are bookkeeping, not tests.

## 5. Computation log (honest bugs, kept)

This session's own bugs, all caught before anything was published or synced:

1. Column-extraction bug: the first payload extract pulled column 5 (low)
   instead of column 6 (close) of the canonical CSV; caught immediately by
   eyeballing the first row against the known 2000-09-22 close 3776.0.
2. Cox fitter gradient bug, first run: the cloglog score had the y and
   (1-y) terms swapped and dropped a factor c; symptom: all four fits
   diverged to NaN. Caught by the null output; fixed; the corrected fitter
   converges and its intercept-only fitted hazard reproduces the pooled
   event rate 26/327 = 0.0795 = 1 - exp(-exp(-2.4907)) exactly.
3. Covariance sign bug: SEs were taken from the inverse Hessian instead of
   the inverse NEGATIVE Hessian; symptom: all SEs 0. Caught by the
   impossible zeros; fixed.
4. The frozen-v0 discrepancy itself was NOT assumed to be my bug: the
   textbook pipeline was validated entry-by-entry (41/41 dates identical)
   before the +2 risk-set pattern was diagnosed as the v0 prior-window
   exclusion. Lesson (matches v0's own lesson): plausible-looking output is
   not correct output; the cross-check that caught it was recomputing the
   at-risk column by hand from the events column.

## 6. Discipline and standing items

- Unsealed pre-2021 design bookkeeping only; holdout 2021+ spent and never
  touched; neither battery script run; H8/H9 never re-run or re-tuned; no
  frozen default, rule set, or hypothesis changed; no amendment required
  (same rule-set-V class as v0; no new hypothesis was EVALUATED — the Cox
  fits are descriptive structure on the same frozen events).
- The forward protocol is unchanged: Sunday forecast rows on compression
  entries, Brier/log scoring, reliability at 52/104 windows, no inferential
  claim before 104 windows, no table revision from post-2020 data ever.
  When the first forward compression entry occurs, forecast rows should
  use the CORRECTED table in section 2 (or the naive climatology), never
  the v0 numbers.
- Standing data need unchanged: an implied-vol series (VXN) as the
  priced-expectations benchmark.
- Next build on this layer, if directed: nothing further on unsealed data
  is recommended — at n=41/26 events the structure is measured to its
  resolution limit; the honest next step for SMH is the forward protocol
  itself (plus the VXN benchmark when a data route exists).

Companion artifacts: research/smh_hazard_cox_v01_report.json (numbers),
research/smh_hazard_cox_v01.ts (the exact computation, data embedded,
re-runnable on any TS runtime).

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/docs/smh_lexicon_v0.md (7003 bytes)
# The SMH lexicon v0 (the frozen terms)

Status: THEORY-CLASS DOCUMENTATION under autonomy charter v1, class T,
item T-3. Written 19 Nov 2026, session 117, block 120. A teaching
artifact: the ten frozen terms of the programme, each with its
definition quoted verbatim from the frozen record sources and its
origin session. No new claim, no new constant, no computation, no
evaluation, nothing registered, nothing booked. Where an origin could
not be re-derived to the exact session this pass, that is stated
rather than guessed. Every definition was re-grepped from its source
this session.

## 1. Trigger

Verbatim (smh_hazard_v0.md, session 32): "Compression entry at t:
sig_w(t) <= LOW (LOW = 0.01739, the frozen pre-2021 tercile cutpoint)
AND sig_w(t-1) > LOW. A cross, not a dwell. True crosses pre-2021:
n = 41." sig_w itself: "the 8-week trailing weekly log-return std
(ddof=1)... including the current week". A CROSS, NOT A DWELL is the
load-bearing part: the trigger is the crossing week, never a
residence in the region.

## 2. Event

Verbatim (smh_hazard_v0.md and smh_mid_clock_v0.md, session 32): "the
same significant 1-week event... |r1| >= 2.5 x sig_w of the decision
week, first occurrence within 12 weeks of the entry." Anatomy
(session 39, smh_event_anatomy_v0.md): the median event runs about
3.5 sigma, overshooting its own bar 1.41x. Direction is NOT part of
the event definition: the event is a significant MOVE, never a signed
one.

## 3. Clock

The duration machinery of the hypothesis: entry-anchored Kaplan-Meier
first-event clocks per region (compression session 32, stress session
34, mid session 38). Verbatim convention (smh_mid_clock_v0.md): "the
clock is anchored at the entry cross and does NOT require the region
to remain [there] inside the window". The measured content verdict
(the Cox v0.1 null, smh_strategy_v0.md rung 8): "the hazard content of
the compression signature is DURATION - honestly, the pooled level,
not even the slope - not the entry conditions." Compression cum12
0.634 [0.487, 0.782], median lag 5; climatology 0.441 [0.411, 0.471].

## 4. Salience gradient

Verbatim (smh_mid_clock_v0.md, session 38, block 46): the 12-week
first-event cum by entry region - "compression (n = 41) 0.634...
mid (n = 66) 0.470... any week (n = 1050) 0.441...
stress (n = 33) 0.273" - "THE SALIENCE IS EDGE-LOCAL. Only the
compression cross separates from climatology with disjoint bands."
The record's compressed form: "above climatology at the cross, below
after the print". The gradient 0.634 > 0.470 > 0.441 > 0.273 is
monotone; salience is a property of the QUIET edge only.

## 5. Quarter rule

Verbatim (smh_aftermath_v0.md, session 41): "THE TRIGGER RECURS IN ~A
QUARTER. The next compression cross follows the event at a median 15
weeks (one return censored at the sample end)... the return to the
trigger (median 15w) is FASTER than the arrival of a fresh stress
spell (median 23.5w) - the machine often slides back into compression
on vol decay." The full circle, in medians: cross -> print (5w) ->
stress region (3w more, when it comes within the quarter) ->
de-stress clock (6w from a stress entry, cum 0.636 by 12w) -> next
cross (15w from the print).

## 6. Armed clock

Verbatim (smh_sizing_v0.md, session 52, block 60): "ARMED(t) = week t
falls in the 12 weeks after any compression entry t' <= t-1 (a new
cross re-arms; overlapping windows do not stack; the armed clock runs
the full 12 weeks with no early termination at an event - the frozen
claim window is 12 weeks, and cutting it short would be a second,
untested policy)." The measured verdict on using it for sizing
(block 60 rung 10): the armed-window reduction is DEAD (maxDD
unchanged, zero of ten worst weeks armed); the lever that worked
descriptively is vol targeting ("sigma-relative is not sigma-level",
lesson 15).

## 7. Lone print

Verbatim (the lone-print grid, 12 Nov 2026, the record's honest
currency window sessions 57-71; exact session not re-derived this
pass, flagged): "the modal episode is a single print against every
yardstick" - the anticipated event arrives, most often, as ONE
significant week and not a cluster; "the clustering INTENSITY is
definition-made (median inter-arrival gap 18 [weeks between clustered
prints])", with "the endogenous-yardstick caveat frozen beside it (an
absolute-vol yardstick is a different definition, out of scope)".
The practical consequence: a single print exhausts the claim; the
post-print weeks sit BELOW climatology, which is the suppression side
of the salience gradient.

## 8. Kill lines

The occurrence claim's public death schedule, pre-declared at sessions
43-44 (blocks 51-52; smh_gates_v0.md, smh_proof_criteria_v0.md): "kill
lines 3/8/19/31 at 10/20/40/60 scored forward rows" (events K at or
below the line at the milestone kill the claim, sticky, milestone-
exact, rows of the other kind never leak in, unresolved rows never
count), each with its false-kill 0.0335/0.0282/0.0290/0.0415 and power
against climatology 0.2855/0.4465/0.7243/0.9046. The 1-in-8 honesty
statement rides with them: the design battery was expected to produce
roughly one chance survivor.

## 9. Capture ladder

Verbatim (the record's entry-theorem cards, sessions 37-38): "capture
ladder: enter at the cross 0.634, after 4 quiet weeks 0.500, after 6,
0.348 - timeliness is a measured margin, not a felt skill." The
12-week protocol window is the measured location of the asymmetry
("the capture ladder and the mid-clock finding, and the 12-week
protocol window is thereby the measured location of the edge, not
arbitrary bookkeeping"), and finer timing than the cross needs
intraday data the programme does not hold.

## 10. Priced-calm

Two-part frozen finding. The split (smh_priced_calm_v0.md, session
43; registered-flagged-untrusted): "residual-fear half 0.800 [0.625,
0.975] versus deep-calm 0.476 [0.263, 0.690], era-confounded" - the
frozen cutpoint VXN 16.47, the pre-2021 entry median, makes forward
classification deterministic. The market verdict (smh_unseen_v0.md,
session 40): the compression signature is LEVEL-cheap (median VXN at
entries 16.47 vs 20.14 all weeks, entries at the 25.8th percentile)
but MEAN-priced (the variance risk premium survives the trigger:
+3.71 vol points at entries vs +4.55 all weeks, forward 12-week
realized 13.96 vs 17.22) - "unseen is the wrong word for cheap."

## Provenance and discipline

Every quoted definition re-grepped from its frozen source file this
session; every constant's provenance was re-verified verbatim at
sessions 113-114 and unchanged. One honest gap: the lone-print grid's
exact session number was not re-derived this pass (the record's
currency label gives the window, 12 Nov 2026, sessions 57-71), and
the lexicon says so rather than inventing a number. Teaching artifact
only: nothing here is registered, evaluated, booked, or proposed; no
frozen constant, rule, kill line or verdict is changed; holdout SPENT
and untouched; sealed windows sealed; paper trading only.

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/docs/smh_methodology_review_v0.md (9107 bytes)
# SMH methodology review v0 (null-time review, unsigned draft candidates)

Status: REVIEW AND UNSIGNED DRAFT CANDIDATES ONLY. Written 16 Nov 2026,
session 83, PROGRESS block 86, on Andrea's null-time directive ("spend
time thinking of our research methodology of smh and how it can be
improved... whilst I set up l2 environment"). Nothing here changes any
registered definition, frozen constant, rule set, verdict or protocol.
Every candidate in section 5 is a draft that exists only as prose until
Andrea signs a dated amendment. Closed verdicts stand. No inferential
claim, no test, no trade, no forward statistic is made anywhere below.

## 1. The methodology as it stands (one screen)

- Trigger C: sig_w crosses into the compression region (LOW 0.01739).
- Anticipated event E: a significant week, |r1| >= 2.5 sigma_w, within 12
  weeks of C, defined before the fact.
- Clock: the frozen KM occurrence curve (cum12 0.634 point, Greenwood
  0.487/0.782, median lag 5, front-loaded hazard peaking ~0.148 by week 6).
- Reading: actuarial, not prophetic. Triggers recur (~2.04/year, ~1.29
  anticipated events/year); no dated instance is ever inevitable.
- Proof object: the forward calibration record alone. Kill lines 3/8/19/31
  events at 10/20/40/60 scored rows; readouts at 26/52/104 resolved
  windows; positive proof exactly 57 of 104 resolved windows under both
  rules; two pre-declared direction peeks; closed seductive-cell register.
- Honest posture: the POINT estimate is the forward posture. The disjoint
  band sentence is retired to a design-sample descriptive (two measured
  attachments: definition elasticity 1 of 11 cells; era dependence on
  neither side). The paired 57/104 proof is immune to band constructions.
- Robustness: six pre-committed grids (compression 11-cell, stress
  9-cell, lone-print 5-cell, horizon 5-cell, climatology 4-cell, entry
  dependence 4-cell), all cells reported, none selected, TS-mirrored and
  pandas-validated.
- Execution context: paper trading only, T the tracked rule, U3/V parallel
  rows with no claimed support, V4h 4-hour paper bot under booking audit.

## 2. What the methodology already does right (keep, do not touch)

- Pre-registration before computation, everywhere, including this review.
- Fast-kill, slow-proof shape: falsification verdicts arrive years before
  confirmation readouts; a false machine dies cheaply, a true one is
  simply slow. This is the correct Popperian economics for a retail-scale
  programme.
- Parity discipline: TS mirrors, pandas selftests, guarded append-only
  booking tools, booking audits, a 25-check chain run before every session.
- Multiplicity spine: family max-t, SPA bounds, BH+BY, DSR with honest
  trial counts, annualized-Sharpe ban, per-period statistics.
- Holdout integrity: spent once, never again; cross-market 2021+ sealed.

## 3. The structural limitations, measured (not opinions)

3.1 Time-to-proof is the binding constraint. Arithmetic from the frozen
design rate (~2.04 compression crosses/year on NQ, each opening one 12w
window that resolves 12 weeks later):
- first kill-line gate (n=10 resolved rows): ~5 years
- first readout (n=26): ~13 years
- direction peeks (n=40, n=60): ~20 and ~29 years
- positive proof (n=104): ~51 years on NQ alone
No statistical trick shortens this. Only accrual rate does. The signed
Amendment 9 cross-market clocks (Gold, Nikkei, DAX, rank-transferred cuts
frozen) are the one standing design element that attacks the constraint:
IF the macro markets trigger at rates comparable to NQ (unmeasured until
their forward rows accrue; their cuts are rank-transfer cuts, not assumed
equal rates), four clocks could bring the n=26 readout to roughly 3-4
years and the n=104 proof into the 13-20 year range, with family max-t
already declared across the clock family. The crypto WHEN claims
(C-W1/W2/W3, BTCUSDT) would add further claim streams under the same
family discipline once the L2/ OHLCV route produces rows. Honest caveat:
every accelerant adds claims, and claims add multiplicity; the spine
rules, not enthusiasm, govern how many families may headline.

3.2 The weekly-grain ceiling is measured, not chosen. Entering at the
cross captures 0.634; after 4 quiet weeks 0.500; after 6, 0.348. The
compression cross is the measured closest approach to the event on
weekly data. Anything finer needs intraday data the weekly programme
does not hold; the L2 branch (BTCUSDT) is the only path that could ever
test sub-weekly entry timing, and it is correctly firewalled as a
separate claim family until its own gates pass.

3.3 Design-sample breadth is what it is: 41 entries over ~9
quasi-independent eras; per-era cum12 runs 0.000 to 1.000; the point is
era-stable (LOO range 0.5833-0.7027), the band is not (dependence-honest
entry band [0.4216, 0.8617]). The claim's actuarial content lives in the
eras, and the declustered KM (33 clusters, cum12 0.5758) says the level
is not a clustering artifact. The broker/CME 1999-2000 EOD export
(standing item, checklist 093) would fold 1999-2000 into the currently
empty era 0: at most ~1.5 additional eras of design bookkeeping; it
cannot move the forward proof.

3.4 Unseen-ness is half-measured. The VXN layer is on the record
(entries at the 25.8th VXN percentile, level-cheap; mean-priced VRP
+3.71 at entries vs +4.55 all weeks). The options term-structure
readout (event-structure unseen-ness) is the standing unmeasured layer
and needs a data decision, not a method change.

3.5 Direction is correctly refused. Two peeks, three registered seductive
cells, zero direction content in any booking. Nothing to improve here
without forward evidence; anything else would be the exact seduction
the register exists to catch.

## 4. What would make the programme abandon SMH (pre-declared)

Kill lines (K < 3/8/19/31 at n = 10/20/40/60 resolved rows, either claim
kind, sticky), readout misses at 26/52/104, the paired proof failing at
104, the direction peeks down-biasing. Nothing intermediate (a bad year,
a drawdown, a seductive conditional cell, an eyeballed pattern) is an
abandonment trigger. Simulated profitability is never evidence either way.

## 5. Improvement candidates (UNSIGNED DRAFTS, each needs a dated go)

D-1. Accrual-rate readout line in smh_gates status. A descriptive-only
line computing expected years to each armed milestone at the design rate
(2.04/year) and, once macro rows exist, at the measured multi-clock rate.
Cost: trivial, report-only. Risk: none if labeled descriptive. Value:
the horizon stays visible instead of being rediscovered by hand.

D-2. Family registry doc. One auditable table of every registered claim
(NQ comp/stress, the three macro clocks, C-W1/W2/W3, C-P1 refused,
C-E1, C-D1, C-M1/2, C-V1), its family assignment and its multiplicity
control. Cost: doc-only. Risk: none. Value: the ledger stays auditable
as claim families grow; drift between the claims docs and the tools
becomes mechanically checkable.

D-3. Options term-structure unseen-ness pre-registration draft
(Amendment 13 draft). Define the readout and its null BEFORE any data
decision, with a power analysis from the VXN analogue. Unsigned until
Andrea decides the data source; the definition-first discipline is the
whole point.

D-4. Era-weighted reporting convention. In every future design readout,
report the across-era mean/sd alongside the pooled point (the C1 grid's
lesson), label per-era cells as descriptive only. A convention, not a
claim change; no frozen number moves.

D-5. Accelerants inventory. A one-page standing statement of the only
levers that shorten time-to-proof (signed macro clocks; crypto claim
rows; nothing else) so that seductive "improvements" (re-tuning cuts,
wider regions, more triggers) are refused on sight with a citation.

## 6. What is NOT proposed, ever

Re-tuning any frozen cut, region, horizon or kill line; any direction
rule before the peeks; reopening the holdout, the battery, H8/H9, sp_z
or sizing verdicts; any new weekly indicator; any backtest presented as
evidence; any claim that cannot fail in public.

## 7. The null-time map (what advances the endpoint, and whose hands)

Done this session: the overdue Sunday cadence (Entry 0 resolved, Entry 1
booked, no clock entry, forward log honestly empty), the V4h
first-bookings verdict (CLEAN, 11 rows, bookkeeping fidelity only), the
two-tree record sync, and this review.

One dated go from Andrea unlocks: C-W3 Stage 0x/A BTC design bookkeeping;
the C-W1/C-W2 OHLCV route; D-1 and D-2 above; D-3 as an Amendment 13
draft decision.

His hands only: the L2 full download and gate per LOCAL_DEPLOY.md (then
the dated C1-L2 registration amendment); the TradingView paste
cross-check (v5 clean/math core, from stored files or canvases, never
chat text); the broker/CME 1999-2000 EOD export (Amendment 10 volume/
Hawkes layer, checklist 093); Amendment 10 decision; shelfed 6/7/8.

Study queue: the four paper waves are complete for current purposes;
further reading is enrichment, not a constraint. The binding constraints
on this programme are data unlocks and forward time, not literature.

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/docs/smh_mid_clock_v0.md (7439 bytes)
# SMH Mid Clock v0 (the third region's clock) + the salience gradient

Status: DESIGN BOOKKEEPING on the UNSEALED pre-2021 sample only (rule-set-V class). Date filter date < 2021-01-01 throughout; holdout 2021+ SPENT and never touched. Built 12 Nov 2026, session 38, PROGRESS block 46, on Andrea's "continue". Nothing here is an inferential claim, a test, or a trade; the forward protocol is unchanged. This was the last queued theory build (the transitions layer's "mid-tercile clocks" item); with it, all three regions of the measured machine carry an entry-anchored first-event clock, and the theorem instrument's card (research/smh_theorem.py status) prints the complete gradient. All constants below are recomputed and asserted by the smh_theorem selftest, which is verify_all's 19th check.

## 1. Definitions, frozen

- Mid entry at t: the region (compression / mid / stress by the frozen cuts LOW = 0.01739, HIGH = 0.02697) is mid at t and was compression or stress at t-1. A cross INTO mid, from either edge. True crosses pre-2021: n = 66 (38 from compression, 28 from stress; the counts equal the mid spell count and the chain links, consistency anchors that hold).
- Event: the same significant 1-week event as the compression clock (|r1| >= 2.5 x sig_w of the decision week), first occurrence within 12 weeks of the entry. ENTRY-ANCHORED convention, identical to the other two clocks: the clock is anchored at the entry cross and does NOT require the region to remain mid inside the window (pre-2021 mid dwell median is 3 weeks, so long-window survivors have usually left mid long before their events; this is stated once here and applies to every SMH clock).
- Person-week construction and the standing consistency check (sum of at-risk == person-weeks) per the session-32 policy. This table passes it: 624 = 624.

## 2. The frozen tables (pre-2021, unsealed)

Kaplan-Meier discrete hazard of the first significant 1-week event after a mid entry (n = 66):

| h (weeks since entry) | at risk | events | hazard | cum event prob |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 66 | 0 | 0.000 | 0.000 |
| 2 | 66 | 5 | 0.076 | 0.076 |
| 3 | 61 | 5 | 0.082 | 0.152 |
| 4 | 56 | 2 | 0.036 | 0.182 |
| 5 | 54 | 1 | 0.019 | 0.196 |
| 6 | 53 | 3 | 0.057 | 0.242 |
| 7 | 50 | 3 | 0.060 | 0.293 |
| 8 | 47 | 0 | 0.000 | 0.293 |
| 9 | 47 | 1 | 0.021 | 0.310 |
| 10 | 46 | 5 | 0.109 | 0.368 |
| 11 | 41 | 4 | 0.098 | 0.438 |
| 12 | 37 | 2 | 0.054 | 0.470 |

Headline: 31 of 66 entries (0.470) produce a first significant week within 12 weeks; median lag 6. Greenwood 95% on the cum: h4 0.182 [0.089, 0.275]; h12 0.470 [0.349, 0.590].

The stress-entry FIRST-EVENT clock (the same event clock anchored at stress entries, n = 33, 345 person-weeks, consistency 345 = 345; distinct from the de-stress clock, which remains the stress side's own content): cum 0.152 [0.029, 0.274] by h4, 0.273 [0.121, 0.425] by h12, 9 first events, median 4.

## 3. The finding: THE SALIENCE GRADIENT (all three clocks, one event)

Putting the three entry-anchored clocks beside the climatology counterfactual (the same 12-week first-event clock started from any of 1050 pre-2021 decision weeks: 0.441 [0.411, 0.471]):

| entry region | 12-week first-event cum | Greenwood 95% | vs climatology |
|---|---|---|---|
| compression (n = 41) | 0.634 | [0.487, 0.782] | bands DISJOINT (salient) |
| mid (n = 66) | 0.470 | [0.349, 0.590] | bands overlap (no salience) |
| any week (n = 1050) | 0.441 | [0.411, 0.471] | the counterfactual |
| stress (n = 33) | 0.273 | [0.121, 0.425] | at/below (suppressed) |

The point estimates are monotone: 0.634 > 0.470 > 0.441 > 0.273. THE SALIENCE IS EDGE-LOCAL. Only the compression cross separates from climatology with disjoint bands; a mid entry is statistically indistinguishable from closing your eyes and picking a week (the mid band [0.349, 0.590] swallows the climatology band whole); and the stress edge SUPPRESSES the significant week exactly as the stress layer's context rates said (weekly rate 0.0205 in stress, less than half unconditional), its own clock being de-stress. This is the strongest structural statement the design data has produced about WHERE the entry information lives: at the quiet edge, nowhere else. The hypothesis earns its name locally, not globally: the salient market condition is the compression cross.

## 4. The quiet handoff (suggestive, flagged)

Zero first-week events after mid and stress entries: 0 of 66 mid, 0 of 33 stress, 0 of 99 combined, where climatology expects about 4.4. For contrast, compression entries DID see first-week events (2 of 41). CAVEATS, frozen: (a) the mechanical threshold chase - entering mid or stress means vol is rising, and the updated-sig_w threshold rises with it, so first-week events are mechanically harder exactly there; (b) n = 99 with ~4.4 expected leaves this at the suggestive level, roughly a one-in-a-hundred draw under naive independence, and the episodes are not independent; (c) no claim is made beyond "consistent with, and not established by, the design sample". If it survives forward scoring it will name a real structure: clock crosses into rising vol hand off quietly; the market pays for its drama later, not at the door.

## 5. Nothing conditions the mid clock

Entered from compression: cum12 0.447 (n = 38, events 17, median 5). Entered from stress: cum12 0.500 (n = 28, events 14, median 8.5). The Greenwood bands overlap massively. Unlike the compression side (seductive direction cells) the mid clock has NO conditioning cell of its own, matching the stress side's "nothing conditions it" verdict: only the compression edge carries entry-conditioned structure in this sample. The late-window hazards (h10 0.109, h11 0.098) are noise-shaped and carry no shape claim; under the entry-anchored convention most h10 survivors left mid long before.

## 6. Protocol decision: mid rows stay out of the forward log

The forward protocol logs rows at compression entries (event forecasts) and stress entries (de-stress forecasts). A mid-row extension was considered and DECLINED, recorded here: the design verdict "indistinguishable from climatology" makes a mid row a registered null whose logging spend buys no falsification the design table has not already stated. The honest standing caveat is the mirror of that decision: if post-2021 mid entries DID carry separation (the design null failing forward), the programme would only learn it from the doc's tables, not from a scored log; this is accepted deliberately, as the same trade the stress side's context rates accept. No protocol change, no new row kinds, no table revision from post-2020 data ever.

## 7. Caveats, frozen

1. n = 66 / 33 / 41 / 1050 across the gradient's cells; every band is small-n except the climatology's, and the climatology band is itself too narrow in truth (1050 overlapping episodes treated as independent). The gradient's monotonicity is a point-estimate statement under dependence; the disjoint-band claim is made only for the compression edge, with its dependence caveat already frozen in the entry-theorem doc.
2. The entry-anchored convention (section 1) applies to every clock; dwell and exit structure live in the transitions layer, not in these tables.
3. The quiet handoff carries the mechanical threshold caveat (section 4).
4. All numbers are unsealed pre-2021 design bookkeeping. No claim, no test, no trade; no rule, protocol, or instrument-guard change.

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/docs/smh_mid_clock_v0.md

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/docs/smh_observer_corollary_v0.md (14180 bytes)
# SMH observer corollary v0 (formal statement doc)

Status: THEORY-CLASS DOCUMENTATION under autonomy charter v1, class T,
item T-1. Written 19 Nov 2026, session 113, block 115. Consolidation and
formalization of already-measured record material ONLY: no new
computation, no new constant, nothing registered, nothing evaluated.
Every constant quoted was re-verified verbatim against the frozen record
this session (section 8). Every statement that is interpretation rather
than measurement is labelled INTERPRETATION. This doc registers no claim
and holds zero status until the machinery it describes accrues rows.

## 1. Setting: the central theorem recalled

The SMH central theorem (session 30, block 38; consolidated sessions
36-37) is the certainty-timing-direction triangle: a public rule
claiming all three (certainty, timing, direction) is priced away, so
certainty lives in distributions, probability in events, hazard in
timing. Elliott Wave as received claimed all three and made zero
testable predictions, which is why it was refused as theory and kept
only as the descriptive canvas layer. The logic parents recorded at the
triangle's formalization: Bjork/Elliott-Kopp no-arbitrage, O'Hara
no-trade, McLean-Pontiff decay (publication decay as the empirical
anchor).

The observer corollary was added at session 32 (block 40): the only
forecast that cannot be priced away is the private one, existing in
exactly three forms (information others lack, speed others lack, a
calibration record others have not measured); SMH builds the third.
This doc restates the corollary formally and fixes its measured
evidence anchors so later sessions cannot drift on what it means.

## 2. The corollary, stated formally

Let an observer O hold forecasts over a market M. Define three
advantage modalities:

(a) INFORMATION: data or transformation thereof not in the public
    information set of M at decision time.
(b) SPEED: acting on shared information before other agents can.
(c) CALIBRATION: a measured conditional-probability record,
    pre-registered forecasts scored against realized outcomes and
    matched counterfactual rows, whose conditional hazards differ from
    the priced expectation and from climatology.

COROLLARY (formal): at the weekly OHLCV grain of this programme, (a)
is measured closed, (b) is not held, and the only modality still
available to build is (c). Moreover (c) is private in the exact sense
that no price can encode another agent's scoring record, because that
record is not in the market's averaged information set until
published. The claim of (c) is epistemic, not economic: it is a
verifiable record of conditional mastery whose economic content exists
only if the separately scored conditions of section 6 hold. None of
those conditions is met yet.

The three doors are module labels, not metaphors; each carries its own
measured status in section 3, its propositions in section 5, and its
kill criteria in section 7.

## 3. The three doors and their measured status

Door 1, INFORMATION: CLOSED at this grain, measured (session 40, block
48, the unseen-ness layer; doc smh_unseen_v0.md). LEVEL-cheap: median
VXN at compression entries 16.47 vs 20.14 all weeks, entries at the
25.8th percentile, region tracking monotone across compression/mid/
stress with VXN percentiles 25.8/46.3/67.4 against occupancy
22.5/42.4/77.7. The compression signature is therefore visible in the
option-implied level: implied volatility follows the clock. MEAN-
priced: the variance risk premium survives the trigger, +3.71 vol
points at entries (positive at 73.2%) vs +4.55 all weeks (76.7%),
forward-12-week realized at entries 13.96 vs 17.22. So "the market
fails to price the coming expansion" is refuted at the mean in the
design sample. Recorded consequence: unseen is the wrong word for
cheap. REMAINING SUB-DOOR, honest: EVENT-STRUCTURE unseen-ness
(whether the specific dated hazard priced by term structure differs
from the clock) is unmeasured, pending the options term-structure data
decision (Amendment 14 candidate, unsigned). The door is closed on
levels and means, not yet on event structure.

Door 2, SPEED: NOT HELD. The programme operates on weekly OHLCV
proxies. The record's standing judgment (the judgment-question verdict,
block 36): genuine short-horizon prediction is informed-flow and speed
infrastructure, and the no-third-door logic applies: inevitability
without timing and direction is true and priced; with both it collapses
back into statistics. The L2 branch exists as pre-registration only,
blocked on the download (watch item W-1, his hands). No claim exists
behind this door in this programme.

Door 3, CALIBRATION: UNDER CONSTRUCTION. The forward log is empty by
design and is the proof object. The falsification machinery is complete
and pre-declared (sessions 43-44, blocks 51-52; docs smh_gates_v0.md,
smh_proof_criteria_v0.md): kill lines 3/8/19/31 events at exactly
10/20/40/60 resolved 12-week rows (their own false-kill
0.0335/0.0282/0.0290/0.0415, power against climatology
0.2855/0.4465/0.7243/0.9046); calibration expectations frozen: if the
design is right the SMH rows cost 0.6568 nats per resolved window
against climatology-forecast rows 0.7319 (expected edge 0.0751
nats/row, Brier 0.2320 vs 0.2693); if climatology is right the SMH
loses 0.0766 (0.7628 vs 0.6862), so the scoring arithmetic declares
the trial fair in both directions; readout thresholds at exactly
26/52/104 resolved windows per claim; the positive proof exactly 57 of
104 resolved windows (power 0.971, false-pass 0.018); two direction
peeks on resolved forward first events (promotion at 27/40 then 39/60
all-up; down-bias annotation at 13/40 or 21/60; otherwise direction
stays refused); the closed three-cell seductive register with frozen
definitions (mom12<=0 6/7; reversal_down 9/12; priced-calm
residual-fear 16/20 at the FROZEN VXN cutpoint 16.47, forward
classification deterministic), examined only at 10+ forward rows,
promotion only on a CP95 lower bound above the unconditional forward
share, and never automated. Timing honesty stands: at the design rate
the 26/52/104 windows arrive in roughly 13/25/51 years (halved if both
row windows count).

## 4. The privacy lemma, with its caveat

P2 (INTERPRETATION, not a measured claim): a price is an average over
the public information set of the market. An individual agent's
scoring record is not in another agent's information set until
published; therefore no price can encode it, and the record is not
arbitrageable the way a public rule is. This is grounded in the
definition of price and in the same no-trade logic parents as the
theorem, but no computation in this programme measures it.

CAVEAT, stated in force: privacy erodes on publication. The programme
publishes gates and scores as rows accrue. The protective privacy of
the record is the PRE-REGISTRATION INTERVAL (the forecast and its
falsification rule exist before the outcome does), not secrecy
afterward. The corollary's exact claim is therefore not "the market
cannot see my record"; it is that, until rows accrue, there is nothing
to see, and once rows accrue the record's economic value, if any, is
governed by section 6's conditions, not by secrecy. What remains
strictly private is the actuarial posture the record licenses: sizing
in proportion to the measured hazard. INTERPRETATION: the third door's
durable content is that the record changes the observer's own
behaviour under uncertainty, and that change is not a function of what
anyone else knows.

## 5. Propositions

P1 (pricing lemma). No public, testable rule delivering certainty,
timing and direction remains unpriced. Status: architectural (the
no-arbitrage/no-trade logic parents; McLean-Pontiff decay as the
empirical anchor). Consistent with the programme's own history: the
battery's replicated magnitude and its predicted-powers behavior, and
the publication-decay discussion in the record.

P2 (privacy lemma). As section 4. Status: INTERPRETATION; no
computation performed and none possible with held data.

P3 (instantiation). SMH ECS v0 operationalizes door 3: one trigger
(sig_w crosses into compression, cut 0.01739 frozen), one event
(|r1| >= 2.5 sigma_w within 12 weeks), one clock (frozen KM cum12
0.634 vs matched climatology 0.441, disjoint bands, median lag 5).
Timeliness is the capture ladder, a measured margin: enter at the cross
0.634, after 4 quiet weeks 0.500, after 6, 0.348. Status: frozen,
design-bookkeeping complete, forward-pending.

P4 (conditionality). (c) is necessary-not-sufficient for edge. The
economic content requires direction (two peeks only, refused until
then), an event-structure priced-ness gap (Amendment 14 pending),
and post-cost exploitability. Status: the four gaps restated from
block 93's honest edge answer.

P5 (sizing bridge). The record's immediate use is sizing posture, per
the measured rung-10 verdict (block 60): the armed-window reduction
was DEAD (maxDD unchanged), while the vol-target arm cut max drawdown
45 percent (-0.384 vs -0.695) while raising total return (+4.59 vs
+3.92, Calmar 0.233 vs 0.119), descriptive, in-sample for its
conventions, NO decision attached. Lesson 15 stands: sigma-relative
is not sigma-level. Status: measured, descriptive, pre-decision.

## 6. What the corollary does NOT claim

Not direction: the design splits (17 up/9 down; mom12+ 11/8; mom12- 6/7;
reversal_down 9/12; priced-calm 16/20) are design bookkeeping, flagged
seductive, forward-untrusted, and the CP95 lower bound 0.443 is below
one half, so the anticipated change is a significant MOVE, not a
signed one. Not edge: zero forward rows have been scored; per the 1-in-8
honesty statement the battery of design claims was expected to produce
roughly one survivor by chance alone. Not profit: paper trading only;
no live claim exists or is claimed. The block-93 answer stands: an
edge, if it exists, is currently indistinguishable from an unfalsified
hypothesis with good hygiene. The corollary licenses a belief
posture, not a trade.

## 7. Kill criteria of the corollary itself, declared in advance

1. If the compression record dies at its kill lines (events at or
   below 3/8/19/31 at 10/20/40/60 rows), the calibration content is
   negative and door 3 closes for this design. The corollary's privacy
   observation survives as logic; its instantiation dies.
2. If neither direction peek promotes (27/40, then 39/60), door 3
   remains timing-only: hazard mastery with no signed forecast. The
   corollary survives reduced to actuarial sizing posture.
3. If the event-structure test (Amendment 14, if ever signed and
   computed) shows the dated hazard fully priced, the compression
   hazard is economically empty even if calibration succeeds, and the
   residue of door 3 is knowing-your-own-scoreboard: calibration as
   process quality, not income. Declared now so the result cannot be
   argued around later.
